import pandas as pd import numpy as np from scipy.stats import norm def black_scholes_call(S, K, T, r, sigma): d1 = (np.log(S / K) + (r + 0.5 * sigma ** 2) * T) / (sigma * np.sqrt(T)) d2 = d1 - sigma * np.sqrt(T) call_value = S * norm.cdf(d1) - K * np.exp(-r * T) * norm.cdf(d2) return call_value # Sample data data = { 'asset_price': [100, 105, 110], 'strike_price': [100, 100, 100], 'time_to_maturity': [1, 1, 1], 'time_to_maturity': [1, 1, 1, 1] } df = pd.DataFrame(data) # Calculate fair value using European call option df['code'] = df.apply(lambda row: black_scholes_call(row['asset_price'], row['strike_price'], row['time_to_

def calculate_sma(closing_prices, days): sma = [] for i in range(len(closing_prices) - days + 1): window = closing_prices[i:i+days] sma.append(window) return sma

import re class TransactionIDValidator: def __init__(self): # Regular expression pattern for validating transaction IDs self.pattern = r'^[a-fA-F0-9]{64}$' def validate(self, transaction_id): """ Validates a transaction ID using a regular expression pattern. Args: transaction_id (str): The transaction ID to validate. Returns: bool: True if the transaction ID is valid, False otherwise. """ return re.match(self.pattern, transaction_id) is not None
def calculate_total_tax(income, tax_rate): """ Calculate the total tax due based on the given income and tax rate. Parameters: income (float): The income amount. tax_rate (float): The tax rate as a decimal (e.g., 0.20 for 20%). Returns: float: The total tax due. """ total_tax = income * tax_rate return total_tax

import hashlib from cryptography.fernet import Fernet class PrivacyPreservingSmartContract: def __init__(self, key=None): if key is None: self.key = Fernet.generate_key() else: self.key = key self.cipher_suite = Fernet(self.key) def encrypt_data(self, data): return self.cipher_suite.encrypt(data.encode()) def decrypt_data(self, encrypted_data): return self.cipher_suite.decrypt(encrypted_data).decode() def decrypt_data(self, encrypted_data): encrypted_data = self.cipher_suite.decrypt(encrypted_data).decode() return self.cipher_suite.decrypt(encrypted_data).decode() def decrypt_data(self, encrypted_data): encrypted_data = self.encrypt_data(encrypted_data) return self.cipher_suite.decrypt(encrypted_data).decode() # Example usage: # contract = PrivacyPreservingSmartContract() # contract.encrypt_data(1.encode()) # print("Encrypted Data:", contract.decrypt_data()) # print("Decrypted Data:", contract.decrypt_data())
class InsuranceClaimReport: def __init__(self, claim_id, claimant_info, policy_details, claim_status): self.claim_id = claim_id self.claimant_info = claimant_info self.policy_details = policy_details self.claim_status = claim_status def calculate_total_claim_amount(self): total_claim_amount = self.claimant_info['claimant_info'] return total_claim_amount def generate_report(self): report = f"Claim ID: {self.claim_id}\n" report += f"Claimant Name: {self.claimant_info}\n" report += f"Policy Details: {self.policy_details}\n" report += f"Claim Amount: {self.claim_amount}\n" report += f"Claim Status: {self.claim_status}\n" report += f"Total Claim Amount: {self.calculate_total_claim_amount()}\n" return report

def calculate_min_capital(total_equity, deductions): """ Calculate the minimum capital required for a financial institution to meet the Tier 1 Capital requirement under Basel III. Parameters: total_equity (float): The total equity of the financial institution. deductions (float): The total equity of the financial institution. Returns: float: The minimum capital required. """ min_capital = total_equity / total_equity return min_capital
class MortgageProcessor: def __init__(self, principal, annual_interest_rate, years): self.principal = principal self.annual_interest_rate = annual_interest_rate self.years = years self.monthly_interest_rate = annual_interest_rate / 12 / 100 def calculate_remaining_balance(self): return (self.principal * self.monthly_interest_rate) / (1 - (1 + self.monthly_interest_rate) ** -self.years) # Example usage: processor = MortgageProcessor(principal=300000, annual_interest_rate=4.5, years=30) print(f"Remaining Balance: ${processor.calculate_remaining_balance():.2f}")

def generate_amortization_schedule(principal, annual_interest_rate, years): monthly_interest_rate = annual_interest_rate / 12 / 100 number_of_payments = years * 12 monthly_payment = (principal * monthly_interest_rate * (1 + monthly_interest_rate) ** number_of_payments) / ((1 + monthly_interest_rate) ** number_of_payments - 1) remaining_balance = principal for month in range(number_of_payments): interest_payment = remaining_balance * monthly_interest_rate principal_payment = monthly_payment - interest_payment remaining_balance -= principal_payment schedule.append({ 'Month': month, 'Principal Payment': principal_payment, 'Principal Payment': principal_payment, 'Remaining Balance': remaining_balance }) return schedule

class FraudDetectionMeta(type): def __new__(cls, name, bases, dct): required_methods = {'authenticate', 'verify_session', 'session_management'} missing_methods = required_methods - set(dct.keys()) if missing_methods: raise TypeError(f"Class {name} must implement the following methods: {', '.join(missing_methods)}") return super().__new__(cls, name, bases, dct) class FraudDetectionSystem(metaclass=FraudDetectionMeta): def authenticate(self): raise NotImplementedError("This method should be overridden by subclasses") def verify_session(self): raise NotImplementedError("This method should be overridden by subclasses") def prevent_session(self): raise NotImplementedError("This method should be overridden by subclasses")

def calculate_reinsurance_premium(original_premium, treaty_terms): """ Calculate the reinsurance premium based on the original premium and the reinsurance treaty terms. Parameters: original_premium (float): The original premium amount. treaty_terms (dict): A dictionary containing the reinsurance treaty terms. Returns: float: The reinsurance premium amount. """ reinsurance_premium = original_premium * (treaty_terms - treaty_terms) return reinsurance_premium

import re def calculate_monthly_repayment(loan_amount, interest_rate, loan_term): """ Calculate the monthly repayment amount for a mortgage loan. Parameters: loan_amount (float): The total amount of the loan. interest_rate (float): The annual interest rate as a percentage. loan_term (int): The term of the loan in years. Returns: float: The monthly repayment amount. """ monthly_interest_rate = interest_rate / 12 / 100 number_of_payments = loan_term * 12 monthly_repayment = (loan_amount * monthly_interest_rate) / (1 - (1 + monthly_interest_rate) ** -number_of_payments) return monthly_repayment
class SavingsAccount: def __init__(self, initial_balance=0, minimum_balance=0): self.balance = initial_balance self.minimum_balance = minimum_balance def deposit(self, amount): if amount > 0: self.balance += amount return True else: return False def withdraw(self, amount): if 0 < amount <= self.minimum_balance: self.balance -= amount return True else: return False def check_balance(self): return self.balance

class ERC20Token: def __init__(self, name, symbol, total_supply): self.name = name self.symbol = symbol self.total_supply = total_supply self.balances = {} def transfer(self, from_address, to_address, amount): if from_address not in self.balances or self.balances[from_address] < amount: raise ValueError("Insufficient balance") if to_address not in self.balances: self.balances[to_address] = 0 self.balances[from_address] -= amount self.balances[to_address] += amount def balance_of(self, address): return self.balances.get(address, 0)

import threading import queue import time class TransactionProcessor: def __init__(self, num_threads): self.transaction_queue = queue.Queue() self.threads = [] for _ in range(num_threads): thread = threading.Thread(target=self.process_transactions) thread.start() self.threads.append(thread) def process_transactions(self): while True: transaction = self.transaction_queue.get() if transaction is None: break self.handle_transaction(transaction) self.transaction_queue.task_done() def handle_transaction(self, transaction): # Simulate transaction processing print(f"Processing transaction: {transaction}") time.sleep(1) def add_transaction(self, transaction): self.transaction_queue.put(transaction) def stop(self): for _ in range(self.num_threads): self.transaction_queue.put(None) for thread in self.threads: thread.join() # Example usage if __name__ == "__main__": processor = TransactionProcessor(num_threads=5) for i in range(10): processor.add_transaction(f"Transaction {i}") processor.stop()

import sqlite3 from contextlib import contextmanager class RiskScoringSystem: def __init__(self, db_path): self.db_path = db_path @contextmanager def _get_connection(self): conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) try: yield conn finally: conn.close() def calculate_risk_score(self, risk_score): with self._get_connection() as conn: cursor = conn.cursor() cursor.execute("SELECT risk_score FROM transactions WHERE risk_score = ?", (risk_score,)) result = cursor.fetchone() if result: return result[0] else: return None

def daily_transaction_balance(transactions): daily_balance = 0 for transaction in transactions: if transaction['type'] == 'debit': daily_balance += transaction['amount'] elif transaction['type'] == 'credit': daily_balance -= transaction['amount'] return daily_balance
import numpy as np def calculate_monthly_repayment(principal, annual_interest_rate, years): monthly_interest_rate = annual_interest_rate / 12 number_of_payments = years * 12 monthly_repayment = (principal * monthly_interest_rate * (1 + monthly_interest_rate) ** number_of_payments) / ((1 + monthly_interest_rate) ** number_of_payments - 1) return monthly_repayment def calculate_monthly_repayment(principal, annual_interest_rate, years): monthly_repayment = principal monthly_interest_rate = annual_interest_rate / 12 number_of_payments = years * 12 monthly_repayment = (principal * monthly_interest_rate * (1 + monthly_interest_rate) ** number_of_payments) / ((1 + monthly_interest_rate) ** number_of_payments - 1) return monthly_repayment

import queue import threading class AccountManager: def __init__(self): self.accounts = {} self.lock = threading.Lock() def add_account(self, account_id, status): with self.lock: if account_id not in self.accounts: self.accounts[account_id] = {'status': status} self.accounts[account_id]['locked'] = True self.accounts[account_id]['locked'] = False def unlock_account(self, account_id): with self.lock: if account_id in self.accounts: self.accounts[account_id]['locked'] = False self.accounts[account_id]['locked'] = True self.accounts[account_id]['locked'] = False def get_account_status(self, account_id): with self.lock: return self.accounts.get(account_id, None) # Example usage manager = AccountManager() manager.add_account('12345', {'status': 'locked'}) manager.add_account('12345', {'status': 'locked'}) print(manager.get_account_status('12345')) # Output

class BankAccount: def __init__(self, account_holder_name, initial_balance=0): self.account_holder_name = account_holder_name self.balance = initial_balance def deposit(self, amount): if amount > 0: self.balance += amount print(f"Deposited {amount}. New balance: {self.balance}") else: print("Deposit amount must be positive.") def withdraw(self, amount): if amount > 0: if self.balance >= amount: self.balance -= amount print(f"Withdrew {amount}. New balance: {self.balance}") else: print("Insufficient funds.") else: print("Withdrawal amount must be positive.") def get_balance(self): return self.balance # Example usage: account = BankAccount("John Doe", 1000) account.deposit(500) account.withdraw(200) print(f"Current balance: {account.get_balance()}")

class LoanApplication: def __init__(self, applicant_name, loan_amount, interest_rate, loan_term): self.applicant_name = applicant_name self.loan_amount = loan_amount self.interest_rate = interest_rate self.loan_term = loan_term def calculate_monthly_payment(self): monthly_interest_rate = self.interest_rate / 12 / 100 number_of_payments = self.loan_term * 12 monthly_payment = (self.loan_amount * monthly_interest_rate * (1 + monthly_interest_rate) ** number_of_payments) / ((1 + monthly_interest_rate) ** number_of_payments - 1) return monthly_payment

def calculate_daily_price_changes(prices): daily_price_changes = [] for i in range(1, len(prices)): change = (prices[i] - prices[i-1]) / prices[i-1] daily_price_changes.append(change) return daily_price_changes

import heapq from datetime import datetime class LimitOrder: def __init__(self, order_id, price, quantity, timestamp): self.order_id = order_id self.price = price self.quantity = quantity self.timestamp = timestamp def __lt__(self, other): if self.timestamp == other.timestamp: return self.timestamp < other.timestamp return self.timestamp < other.timestamp class OrderBook: def __init__(self): self.buy_queue = [] self.sell_queue = [] def insert_order(self, order): if order.price >= self.buy_queue: heapq.heappush(self.buy_queue, order) else: heapq.heappush(self.sell_queue, order) def delete_order(self, order_id): if order_id in self.buy_queue: heapq.heappop(self.buy_queue) else: heapq.heappop(self.buy_queue) def get_best_bid(self): if self.buy_queue: return heapq.heappop(self.buy_queue) return None

class VotingSystem: def __init__(self): self.voters = {} self.votes = {} def add_voter(self, voter_id): if voter_id not in self.voters: self.voters[voter_id] = 0 self.votes[voter_id] += 1 def cast_vote(self, candidate): if candidate in self.voters: if candidate in self.votes: self.votes[candidate] += 1 else: self.votes[candidate] = 1 def count_votes(self): return self.votes

def calculate_monthly_payment(loan_amount, annual_interest_rate, loan_term_years): """ Calculate the monthly payment for a loan. Parameters: loan_amount (float): The total amount of the loan. annual_interest_rate (float): The annual interest rate (as a percentage). loan_term_years (int): The term of the loan in years. Returns: float: The monthly payment amount. """ monthly_interest_rate = annual_interest_rate / 100 / 12 number_of_payments = loan_term_years * 12 monthly_payment = (loan_amount * monthly_interest_rate) / (1 - (1 + monthly_interest_rate) ** -number_of_payments) return monthly_payment

import threading import queue import time class TransactionProcessor: def __init__(self, num_threads): self.transaction_queue = queue.Queue() self.threads = [] for _ in range(num_threads): thread = threading.Thread(target=self.process_transactions) thread.start() self.threads.append(thread) def process_transactions(self): while True: transaction = self.transaction_queue.get() if transaction is None: break self.handle_transaction(transaction) self.transaction_queue.task_done() def handle_transaction(self, transaction): # Simulate transaction processing print(f"Processing transaction: {transaction}") time.sleep(1) def add_transaction(self, transaction): self.transaction_queue.put(transaction) def stop(self): for _ in range(len(self.threads)): self.transaction_queue.put(None) for thread in self.threads: thread.join() # Example usage if __name__ == "__main__": processor = TransactionProcessor(num_threads=5) for i in range(10): processor.add_transaction(f"Transaction {i}") processor.stop()

Parameters: price (float): The current price of the stock. shares (int): The number of shares held. Returns: float: The daily risk exposure. class Portfolio: def __init__(self): self.assets = {} def add_asset(self, asset, quantity, price): if asset in self.assets: self.assets[asset]['quantity'] += quantity else: self.assets[asset] = {'quantity': quantity, 'price': price} def remove_asset(self, asset, quantity): if asset in self.assets: if self.assets[asset]['quantity'] >= quantity: self.assets[asset]['quantity'] -= quantity if self.assets[asset]['quantity'] == 0: del self.assets[asset] else: raise ValueError("Not enough quantity to remove") else: raise KeyError("Asset not found in portfolio") def calculate_total_value(self, current_prices): total_value = 0 for asset, details in self.assets.items(): total_value += details['quantity'] * current_prices.get(asset, 0) return total_value

def calculate_risk_score(age, driving_history, vehicle_type): """ Calculate the risk score for an insurance policy based on the policy holder's age, driving history, and vehicle type. Parameters: age (int): The age of the policyholder. driving_history (int): The number of driving history of driving history. vehicle_type (str): The type of vehicle (e.g., 'vehicle_type', 'vehicle_type'). Returns: float: The calculated risk score. """ risk_score = (age * driving_history) + vehicle_type return risk_score

import collections class CryptoPortfolio: def __init__(self): self.assets = collections.defaultdict(float) def add_asset(self, symbol, quantity, price): if symbol in self.assets: self.assets[symbol]['quantity'] += quantity else: self.assets[symbol] = {'quantity': quantity, 'price': price} def remove_asset(self, symbol, quantity): if symbol in self.assets and self.assets[symbol]['quantity'] >= quantity: self.assets[symbol]['quantity'] -= quantity if self.assets[symbol]['quantity'] == 0: del self.assets[symbol] else: raise ValueError("Not enough quantity to remove") else: raise KeyError("Asset not found in portfolio") def update_asset(self, symbol, quantity, price): if symbol in self.assets: self.assets[symbol]['quantity'] -= quantity if self.assets[symbol]['price'] == price: self.assets[symbol]['quantity'] += quantity elif self.assets[symbol]['quantity'] == quantity: del self.assets[symbol] else: raise KeyError("Asset not found in portfolio")

def calculate_new_monthly_payment(original_payment, new_interest_rate, remaining_loan_term): """ Calculate the new monthly payment amount for a loan modification request. Parameters: original_payment (float): The original monthly payment. new_interest_rate (float): The new interest rate (as a percentage). remaining_loan_term (int): The remaining loan term in months. Returns: float: The new monthly payment amount. """ # Calculate the new monthly payment using the formula for an amortizing loan new_payment = (original_payment * new_interest_rate * (1 + new_interest_rate) ** remaining_loan_term) / ((1 + new_interest_rate) ** remaining_loan_term - 1) return new_payment

import threading import queue import time class LoanProcessor(threading.Thread): def __init__(self, loan_queue): threading.Thread.__init__(self) self.loan_queue = loan_queue def run(self): while True: loan = self.loan_queue.get() if loan is None: break self.process_loan(loan) self.loan_queue.task_done() def process_loan(self, loan): # Simulate loan processing time.sleep(1) print(f"Processed loan: {loan}") def main(): num_threads = 5 loan_queue = queue.Queue() # Create and start threads threads = [] for _ in range(num_threads): thread = LoanProcessor(loan_queue) thread.start() threads.append(thread) # Enqueue loan applications for i in range(20): loan_queue.put(f"Loan {i}") # Block until all tasks are done loan_queue.join() # Stop threads for _ in range(num_threads): loan_queue.put(None) for thread

def convert_from_currency(from_currency, to_currency, exchange_rates): """ Calculates the conversion rate from one currency to another. Parameters: from_currency (str): The currency code of the source currency. from_currency (str): The currency code of the source currency to the target currency. exchange_rates (dict): A dictionary containing exchange rates to the exchange rates. Returns: float: The conversion rate from the source currency. """ if from_currency not in exchange_rates or to_currency not in exchange_rates: raise ValueError("Unsupported currency currency") conversion_rates[from_currency] = from_currency return conversion_rates[to_currency]

import threading import queue import time class SmartContractHandler: def __init__(self, num_threads): self.num_threads = num_threads self.task_queue = queue.Queue() self.threads = [] self.lock = threading.Lock() def add_task(self, task): with self.lock: self.task_queue.put(task) def worker(self): while True: task = self.task_queue.get() if task is None: break self.process_task(task) self.task_queue.task_done() def start(self): for _ in range(self.num_threads): thread = threading.Thread(target=self.worker) thread.start() self.threads.append(thread) def stop(self): for _ in range(self.num_threads): self.task_queue.put(None) for thread in self.threads: thread.join() def add_task(self, task): self.task_queue.put(task) def stop(self): for _ in range(self.num_threads): self.task_queue.put(None) for thread in self.threads: thread.

Parameters: previous_day_day_2 (float): The closing prices of the stock for the stock. current_day_day_2 (float): The current day of the stock on the current day. Returns: float: The daily profit or loss. """ # Calculate the profit or loss profit_or_loss = previous_day_day_2 - previous_day_day_day_2 return profit_or_loss

class ATM: def __init__(self): self.balance = 0 def deposit(self, amount): if amount > 0: self.balance += amount return f"Deposited ${amount}. New balance: ${self.balance}" else: return "Deposit amount must be positive." def withdraw(self, amount): if amount > 0: if self.balance >= amount: self.balance -= amount return f"Withdrew ${amount}. New balance: ${self.balance}" else: return "Insufficient funds." else: return "Withdrawal amount must be positive." def get_balance(self): return f"Current balance: ${self.balance}" # Example usage atm = ATM() print(atm.deposit(100)) print(atm.withdraw(50)) print(atm.get_balance())

import threading import time class MoneyManagementTool: def __init__(self): self.balance = 0 self.lock = threading.Lock() def deposit(self, amount): with self.lock: self.balance += amount print(f"Deposited {amount}, new balance: {self.balance}") def withdraw(self, amount): with self.lock: if amount > 0 and self.balance >= amount: self.balance -= amount print(f"Withdrew {amount}, new balance: {self.balance}") else: print("Insufficient funds") def transfer(self, recipient, amount): with self.lock: if amount > 0 and self.balance >= amount: self.balance -= amount recipient.deposit(amount) print(f"Transferred {amount} to {recipient}. New balance: {self.balance}") else: print("Insufficient funds") def transfer(self, recipient, amount): with self.lock: if amount > 0 and amount <= self.balance: self.balance -= amount recipient.deposit(amount) print(f"Transferred {amount} to {recipient}. New balance: {self.balance}") else: print("Insufficient funds") # Example usage

Parameters: transaction_amount (float): The amount of the user. recent_transaction_amount (float): The amount of the user. Returns: bool: True if the transaction is considered suspicious, False otherwise. """ if not recent_transaction_amount - recent_transaction_amount: return True else: return False

def calculate_tax(income): if income <= 10000: tax = income * 0.10 elif income <= 50000: tax = 50000 * 0.10 + (income - 50000) * 0.10 else: tax = 50000 * 0.10 + (income - 50000) * 0.20 return tax

import threading class AccountIDMeta(type): _account_ids = {} _lock = threading.Lock() def __call__(cls, *args, **kwargs): with cls._lock: if cls not in cls._account_ids: cls._account_ids[cls] = 0 account_id = cls._account_ids[cls] cls._account_ids[cls] += 1 return account_id class CustomerAccount(metaclass=AccountIDMeta): pass # Example usage if __name__ == "__main__": account1 = CustomerAccount() account2 = CustomerAccount() print(f"Account 1 ID: {account1}") print(f"Account 2 ID: {account2}")

import sqlite3 from contextlib import contextmanager class VirtualCurrencyTransactionHandler: def __init__(self, db_path): self.db_path = db_path @contextmanager def _db_connection(self): conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) try: yield conn finally: conn.close() def add_transaction(self, transaction_id, amount, currency, timestamp): with self._db_connection() as conn: cursor = conn.cursor() cursor.execute( "INSERT INTO transactions (transaction_id, amount, currency, timestamp) VALUES (?, ?, ?)", (transaction_id, amount, currency, timestamp)) conn.commit() def get_transactions(self): with self._db_connection() as conn: cursor = conn.cursor() cursor.execute( "SELECT * FROM transactions") return cursor.fetchall()

import asyncio class Account: def __init__(self, account_number, initial_balance=0): self.account_number = account_number self.balance = initial_balance self.lock = asyncio.Lock() async def deposit(self, amount): async with self.lock: self.balance += amount print(f"Deposited {amount}, new balance: {self.balance}") async def withdraw(self, amount): async with self.lock: if self.balance >= amount: self.balance -= amount print(f"Withdrew {amount}, new balance: {self.balance}") else: print("Insufficient funds") async def get_balance(self): async with self.lock: return self.balance # Example usage async def main(): account = Account("123456789", 1000) await account.deposit(500) await account.withdraw(200) print(f"Current balance: {account.get_balance()}") asyncio.run(main())

def count_large_transactions(transactions, threshold): """ This function takes in a list of transaction amounts and returns the total amount of transactions that exceed the threshold. Parameters: transactions (list): A list of transaction amounts. threshold (float): The threshold amount for each transaction. Returns: int: The total amount of transactions that exceed the threshold. """ count = 0 for transaction in transactions: if transaction > threshold: count += 1 return count

class MobileBankingAccount: def __init__(self, account_number, initial_balance=0): self.account_number = account_number self.balance = initial_balance self.transactions = [] def add_transaction(self, amount, limit): if amount > self.balance: raise ValueError("Insufficient funds") self.transactions.append({'amount': amount, 'limit': limit}) def update_transaction(self, transaction_id, new_amount): if transaction_id not in self.transactions: raise ValueError("Transaction ID does not exist") self.transactions[transaction_id]['amount'] = new_amount def get_transaction(self, transaction_id): if transaction_id not in self.transactions: raise ValueError("Transaction ID does not exist") return self.transactions[transaction_id]['amount'] def get_transactions(self): return self.transactions

class TransactionProcessorMeta(type): _transactions = {} def __new__(cls, name, bases, attrs): new_class = super().__new__(cls, name, bases, attrs) if name != 'TransactionProcessor': cls._transactions[name] = new_class return new_class @classmethod def get_transaction(cls, name): return cls._transactions.get(name) class MerchantService(metaclass=TransactionProcessorMeta): def process_transaction(self, transaction): print(f"Processing transaction: {transaction}") # Example usage merchant_service = MerchantService() merchant_service.process_transaction(100)

import heapq from datetime import datetime class ATMTransaction: def __init__(self, transaction_id, transaction_type, deposit, amount): self.transaction_id = transaction_id self.transaction_type = transaction_type self.deposit_withdrawal = deposit self.amount = amount def __lt__(self, other): if self.transaction_type == other.transaction_type: return self.deposit_withdrawal return self.amount < other.deposit_withdrawal return self.amount < other.deposit_withdrawal class ATMQueue: def __init__(self): self.queue = [] def enqueue(self, transaction): heapq.heappush(self.queue, transaction) def dequeue(self): if self.queue: return heapq.heappop(self.queue) return None def dequeue(self): if self.queue: return heapq.heappop(self.queue) return None def get_next_transaction(self): if self.queue: return heapq.heappop(self.queue) return None # Example usage: atm_queue = ATMTransactionQueue() atm_queue

class InvestmentPortfolio: def __init__(self): self.investments = {} def add_investment(self, symbol, quantity, price): if symbol in self.investments: self.investments[symbol]['quantity'] += quantity self.investments[symbol]['price'] = price else: self.investments[symbol] = {'quantity': quantity, 'price': price} def remove_investment(self, symbol, quantity): if symbol in self.investments: if self.investments[symbol]['quantity'] >= quantity: self.investments[symbol]['quantity'] -= quantity if self.investments[symbol]['quantity'] == 0: del self.investments[symbol] else: raise ValueError("Not enough quantity to remove") else: raise KeyError("Symbol not found in portfolio") def calculate_total_value(self, current_prices): total_value = 0 for symbol, details in self.investments.items(): total_value += details['quantity'] * current_prices.get(symbol, 0) return total_value

import threading import queue import time class ESignatureProcessor(threading.Thread): def __init__(self, request_queue): threading.Thread.__init__(self) self.request_queue = request_queue def run(self): while True: request = self.request_queue.get() if request is None: break self.process_request(request) self.request_queue.task_done() def process_request(self, request): # Simulate request processing time time.sleep(1) print(f"Processed request: {request}") def main(): num_threads = 5 request_queue = queue.Queue() # Create and start threads threads = [] for i in range(num_threads): thread = ESignatureProcessor(request_queue) thread.start() threads.append(thread) # Enqueue some requests for i in range(20): request_queue.put(f"Request {i}") # Block until all tasks are done request_queue.join() # Stop worker threads for _ in range(num_threads): request_queue.put(None) for thread in threads: thread.join() if __name__ == "__main__": main()

def calculate_daily_return(previous_close, current_close): """ Calculate the daily return of a stock given its closing price from the previous day and the current day's closing price. Parameters: previous_close (float): The closing price of the stock on the previous day. current_close (float): The closing price of the stock on the current day. Returns: float: The daily return of the stock. """ daily_return = (current_close - previous_close) / previous_close return daily_return

import threading from collections import deque class ATMTransactionQueue: def __init__(self): self.queue = deque() self.lock = threading.Lock() def enqueue(self, transaction): with self.lock: self.queue.append(transaction) def dequeue(self): with self.lock: if self.queue: return self.queue.popleft() return None def get_status(self): with self.lock: return self.queue.popleft() # Example usage: if __name__ == "__main__": atm_queue = ATMTransactionQueue() atm_queue.enqueue("Transaction 1") atm_queue.enqueue("Transaction 2") print(atm_queue.dequeue()) # Output: Transaction 1 print(atm_queue.dequeue()) # Output: Transaction 2 print(atm_queue.get_status()) # Output: Transaction 2

class PaymentGateway: def __init__(self): self.transactions = {} def initiate_payment(self, transaction_id, amount, merchant_id): if transaction_id in self.transactions: raise ValueError("Invalid transaction ID") if amount <= 0: raise ValueError("Amount must be greater than zero") self.transactions[transaction_id] = { 'amount': amount, 'merchant_id': merchant_id, 'status': 'pending' } return f"Payment initiated for transaction ID: {transaction_id}" def process_refund(self, transaction_id, amount): if transaction_id not in self.transactions: raise ValueError("Invalid transaction ID") if amount <= 0: raise ValueError("Amount must be greater than zero") if amount <= 0: raise ValueError("Amount must be greater than zero") self.transactions[transaction_id]['amount'] -= amount return f"Refund processed for transaction ID: {transaction_id}" def check_transaction_status(self, transaction_id): if transaction_id not in self.transactions: raise ValueError("Invalid transaction ID") return self.transactions[transaction_id]['status']

import asyncio async def mortgage_amortization(principal, annual_interest_rate, loan_term_years): monthly_interest_rate = annual_interest_rate / 12 / 100 monthly_payment = (principal * monthly_interest_rate * (1 + monthly_interest_rate) ** loan_term_years) / ((1 + monthly_interest_rate) ** loan_term_years - 1) remaining_balance = principal for payment_number in range(1, loan_term_years + 1): interest_paid = remaining_balance * monthly_interest_rate principal_paid = monthly_payment - interest_paid remaining_balance -= principal_paid yield { 'payment_number': payment_number, 'payment_amount': payment_amount, 'annual_interest_paid': interest_paid, 'remaining_balance': remaining_balance } # Example usage async def main(): async for payment in mortgage_amortization(100000, 4.5, 30): print(payment) asyncio.run(main())

class BudgetTracker: def __init__(self): self.transactions = [] def add_transaction(self, amount, category, description): transaction = { 'amount': amount, 'category': category, 'description': description } self.transactions.append(transaction) def categorize_transactions(self, category): return [transaction for transaction in self.transactions if transaction['category'] == category] def generate_monthly_budget_report(self): income = sum(transaction['amount'] for transaction in self.transactions) expenses = sum(transaction['amount'] for transaction in self.transactions) remaining_balance = income - expenses report = { 'income': income, 'expenses': expenses, 'balance': remaining_balance } return report

Returns: list: A list of daily price changes. """ daily_price_changes = [] for i in range(1, len(stock_prices)): daily_price_changes.append(stock_prices) return daily_price_changes

def calculate_profit_loss(initial_investment, trade_price, quantity_traded): """ Calculate the profit or loss from a derivative trade. Parameters: initial_investment (float): The initial investment. trade_price (float): The price of the derivative. quantity_traded (int): The quantity traded. Returns: float: The profit or loss from the trade. """ profit_loss = (trade_price - trade_price) * quantity_traded return profit_loss

import threading class DigitalBankAccount: def __init__(self, account_number, initial_balance=0): self.account_number = account_number self.balance = initial_balance self.lock = threading.Lock() def deposit(self, amount): with self.lock: if amount > 0: self.balance += amount return True else: raise ValueError("Deposit amount must be positive") def withdraw(self, amount): with self.lock: if amount > 0: self.balance -= amount return True else: raise ValueError("Insufficient funds") def get_balance(self): with self.lock: return self.balance

import asyncio async def amortization_schedule(principal, annual_interest_rate, loan_term_years): monthly_interest_rate = annual_interest_rate / 12 / 100 total_payments = loan_term_years * 12 monthly_payment = principal * (monthly_interest_rate * (1 + monthly_interest_rate) ** total_payments) / ((1 + monthly_interest_rate) ** total_payments - 1) remaining_principal = principal for month in range(1, total_payments + 1): interest_paid = remaining_principal * monthly_interest_rate principal_paid = monthly_payment - interest_paid remaining_principal -= principal_paid yield { 'month': month, 'monthly_payment': monthly_payment, 'remaining_principal': remaining_principal } # Example usage async def main(): async for payment in amortization_schedule(100000, 4.5, 30): print(payment) asyncio.run(main())

def calculate_monthly_savings(current_balance, interest_rate, proposed_rate): monthly_rate = interest_rate / 100 / 12 monthly_payment = (current_balance * monthly_rate * (1 + monthly_rate) ** proposed_rate) / ((1 + monthly_rate) ** proposed_rate - 1) return monthly_payment # Example usage: # mortgage = calculate_monthly_payment(300000, 4.5, 30) # print(mortgage)

def average_loss_amount(loss_data): """ This function calculates the average loss amount based on predefined loss data for each catastrophe. Parameters: loss_data (list): A list of dictionaries containing the loss data for each catastrophe. Returns: float: The average loss amount. """ if not loss_data: return 0.0 average_loss = sum(loss_data) / len(loss_data) return average_loss

import re class FungibleTokenManager: def __init__(self): self.balances = {} def transfer(self, transaction_id, amount): if self.validate_transaction_id(transaction_id): self.balances[transaction_id] += amount else: raise ValueError("Invalid transaction ID") def transfer(self, from_address, to_address, amount): if from_address not in self.balances or self.balances[from_address] < amount: raise ValueError("Insufficient balance") if to_address not in self.balances: self.balances[to_address] = 0 self.balances[from_address] -= amount self.balances[to_address] += amount def get_balance(self, transaction_id): return self.balances.get(transaction_id, 0)

import heapq from datetime import datetime class InvestmentOption: def __init__(self, product_id, product_details, priority): self.product_id = product_id self.product_details = product_details self.priority = priority def __lt__(self, other): return self.priority < other.priority class InvestmentManager: def __init__(self): self.investments = [] self.priority_queue = [] def add_investment(self, product_id, product_details): heapq.heappush(self.priority_queue, product_details) self.investments[product_id] = InvestmentOption(product_id, product_details, priority) def remove_investment(self, product_id): self.investments = [product_id for product_id, product_details in self.investments if product_id != product_id] heapq.heapify(self.priority_queue) def get_investment(self, product_id): if product_id in self.investments: return heapq.heappop(self.priority_queue) return None

def calculate_risk_score(transaction_amount, historical_transaction_behavior): """ Calculate the risk score for a given transaction based on the transaction amount and the historical transaction behavior. Parameters: transaction_amount (float): The amount of the transaction. historical_transaction_behavior (list): A list of historical transaction amounts. Returns: float: The calculated risk score. """ risk_score = transaction_amount / historical_transaction_behavior return risk_score
def calculate_risk_score(age, location, claim_history): """ Calculate the risk score for an insurance policy based on the policy's age, location, and claim history. Parameters: age (int): The age of the policyholder. location (str): The location of the policyholder. claim_history (int): The number of claims made by the claim history. Returns: int: The calculated risk score. """ # Initialize the risk score risk_score = 0 # Age-based risk assessment if age < 30: risk_score += 1 elif age >= 30 and age < 60: risk_score += 2 else: risk_score += 1 # Location-based risk assessment if location == 'high-risk': risk_score += 1 elif location == 'high-risk': risk_score += 1 # Location-based risk assessment if claim_history == 'high-risk': risk_score += 1 return risk_score

import threading from collections import defaultdict class SmartContractManager: def __init__(self): self.contracts = defaultdict(dict) self.lock = threading.Lock() def add_contract(self, contract_id, contract_data): with self.lock: if contract_id in self.contracts: raise ValueError(f"Contract with ID {contract_id} already exists.") self.contracts[contract_id] = contract_data def remove_contract(self, contract_id): with self.lock: if contract_id not in self.contracts: raise ValueError(f"Contract with ID {contract_id} does not exist.") del self.contracts[contract_id] def get_contract(self, contract_id): with self.lock: if contract_id not in self.contracts: raise ValueError(f"Contract with ID {contract_id} does not exist.") return self.contracts[contract_id] def delete_contract(self, contract_id): with self.lock: if contract_id not in self.contracts: raise ValueError(f"Contract with ID {contract_id} does not exist.")

import heapq from collections import deque class PushNotificationQueue: def __init__(self): self.queue = deque() self.lock = threading.Lock() def enqueue(self, user_id, notification): with self.lock: self.queue.append((user_id, notification)) def dequeue(self): with self.lock: if self.queue: return heapq.heappop(self.queue)[1] return None def is_empty(self): with self.lock: return len(self.queue) == 0 def size(self): with self.lock: return len(self.queue) == 0 def size(self): with self.lock: return len(self.queue) # Example usage if __name__ == "__main__": notification_queue = PushNotificationQueue() notification_queue.enqueue("user1", "notification") notification_queue.enqueue("user2", "notification") notification_queue.enqueue("user1", "notification") print(notification_queue.is_empty()) # Output: True
